   10  *   INCLUDE MZMMPREPROLEF01                                            *

   12  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
   13  *&      Form  bac_confirm
   14  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
   15  *       text
   16  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
   17  *  -->  p1        text
   18  *  <--  p2        text
   19  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
   20  FORM bac_confirm.
   21
   22    Data l_choice.
   23    clear l_choice.
   24    IF g_mode <> 'DIS'.
   25  " Begin of <RD1K960036>.
   26  *    CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM_STEP'
   27  *         EXPORTING
   28  *              TEXTLINE1      = 'Data will be lost, Want to quit? '
   29  *              TITEL          = 'BACK'
   30  *              START_COLUMN   = 25
   31  *              START_ROW      = 6
   32  *              CANCEL_DISPLAY = ''
   33  *         IMPORTING
   34  *              ANSWER         = l_choice.
   35
   36      DATA : l_get1(1) TYPE c.
   37      CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
   38        EXPORTING
   39         TITLEBAR                    = 'BACK '
   40          TEXT_QUESTION               = 'Data will be lost, Want to quit?
       '
   41         DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON       = ' '
   42         START_COLUMN                = 25
   43         START_ROW                   = 6
   44       IMPORTING
   45         ANSWER                      = l_get1
   46       EXCEPTIONS
   47         TEXT_NOT_FOUND              = 1
   48         OTHERS                      = 2
   49                .
   50      IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
   51         CASE l_get1.
   52           WHEN '1'.
   53             MOVE 'J' TO l_choice.
   54             WHEN '2'.
   55               MOVE 'N' TO l_choice.
   56               ENDCASE.
   57               ENDIF.
   58  " End of <RD1K960036>.
   59
   60
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page      4
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

   61      If l_choice = 'J'.
   62  *       perform clear_var.
   63        clear l_choice.
   64      endif.
   65    ELSE.
   66  *     perform clear_var.
   67    ENDIF.
   68
   69  ENDFORM.                    " bac_confirm
   70  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
   71  *&      Form  fill_sttab
   72  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
   73  *       text
   74  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
   75  *  -->  p1        text
   76  *  <--  p2        text
   77  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
   78  FORM fill_sttab.
   79
   80    if sy-tcode = 'ZMM_ARMS_CONNECT'.
   81         old_ok_code = 'DISPLAY'.
   82         get parameter id 'ZREQNO' field zmm_prep_rolereq-docno.
   83    endif.
   84
   85    refresh it_tab.
   86    clear wa_tab.
   87
   88    if old_ok_code =  'CREATE' or
   89          old_ok_code = 'CROSSCO' or
   90          old_ok_code = 'CRCROLES' or
   91          old_ok_code =  'CHANGE' or
   92          old_ok_code =  'RELEASE' or
   93          old_ok_code =  'APPROVE' or
   94          old_ok_code = 'DISPLAY'  or
   95          old_ok_code = 'DELETE'.
   96
   97      move 'CREATE' to wa_tab-fcode.
   98      append wa_tab to it_tab.
   99      move 'CHANGE' to wa_tab-fcode.
  100      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  101      move 'DELETE' to wa_tab-fcode.
  102      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  103      move 'DISPLAY' to wa_tab-fcode.
  104      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  105      move 'RELEASE' to wa_tab-fcode.
  106      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  107      move 'APPROVE' to wa_tab-fcode.
  108      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  109      move 'SUIM' to wa_tab-fcode.
  110      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  111      move 'ROLE_DEL' to wa_tab-fcode.
  112      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  113      move 'CROSSCO' to wa_tab-fcode.
  114      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  115      move 'CRCROLES' to wa_tab-fcode.
  116      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  117  *     move 'ATTACH' to wa_tab-fcode.
  118  *     append wa_tab to it_tab.
  119
  120    else.
  121
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page      5
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  122      move 'CHECK' to wa_tab-fcode.
  123      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  124      move 'ATTACH' to wa_tab-fcode.
  125      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  126      move 'LIST' to wa_tab-fcode.
  127      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  128      move 'SAV' to wa_tab-fcode.
  129      append wa_tab to it_tab.
  130
  131    endif.
  132
  133  ENDFORM.                    " fill_sttab
  134  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  135  *&      Form  lock_reqhd
  136  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  137  *       text
  138  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  139  *  -->  p1        text
  140  *  <--  p2        text
  141  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  142  FORM lock_reqhd.
  143
  144    CALL FUNCTION 'ENQUEUE_EZ_MM_PREPHDR'
  145         EXPORTING
  146              MODE_ZMM_CDHD  = 'E'
  147              MANDT          = SY-MANDT
  148              DOCNO          = zmm_prep_rolereq-docno
  149         EXCEPTIONS
  150              FOREIGN_LOCK   = 1
  151              SYSTEM_FAILURE = 2
  152              OTHERS         = 3.
  153
  154    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
  155      MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
  156             WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
  157    ELSE.
  158      MOVE 'Y' to g_lock.
  159    ENDIF.
  160
  161  ENDFORM.                    " lock_reqhd
  162  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  163  *&      Form  get_correspondense
  164  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  165  *       text
  166  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  167  *  -->  p1        text
  168  *  <--  p2        text
  169  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  170  FORM get_correspondense.
  171
  172    DATA : l_cors like THEAD-TDNAME.
  173
  174    IF old_ok_code <> 'CREATE' or
  175       old_ok_code <> 'CROSSCO'.
  176
  177      refresh lines_cors.
  178
  179      move zmm_prep_rolereq-docno to l_cors.
  180
  181      CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
  182           EXPORTING
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page      6
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  183                CLIENT                  = SY-MANDT
  184                ID                      = '0001'
  185                LANGUAGE                = SY-LANGU
  186                NAME                    = l_cors
  187                OBJECT                  = 'ZHELP'
  188           TABLES
  189                LINES                   = lines_cors
  190           EXCEPTIONS
  191                ID                      = 1
  192                LANGUAGE                = 2
  193                NAME                    = 3
  194                NOT_FOUND               = 4
  195                OBJECT                  = 5
  196                REFERENCE_CHECK         = 6
  197                WRONG_ACCESS_TO_ARCHIVE = 7
  198                OTHERS                  = 8.
  199
  200      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
  201        read_flag = ''.
  202        zmm_prep_rolereq-long_text_fl = ''.
  203      Else.
  204        read_flag = 'X'.
  205        zmm_prep_rolereq-long_text_fl = 'X'.
  206      ENDIF.
  207    ENDIF.
  208
  209  ENDFORM.                    " get_correspondense

  211  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  212  *   INCLUDE TABLECONTROL_FORMS                                         *
  213  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  214
  215  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  216  *&      Form  USER_OK_TC                                               *
  217  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  218  FORM USER_OK_TC USING    P_TC_NAME TYPE DYNFNAM
  219                           P_TABLE_NAME
  220                           P_MARK_NAME
  221                  CHANGING P_OK      LIKE SY-UCOMM.
  222
  223  *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
  224    DATA: L_OK              TYPE SY-UCOMM,
  225          L_OFFSET          TYPE I.
  226  *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
  227
  228  * Table control specific operations                                    *
  229  *   evaluate TC name and operations                                    *
  230    SEARCH P_OK FOR P_TC_NAME.
  231    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
  232      EXIT.
  233    ENDIF.
  234    L_OFFSET = STRLEN( P_TC_NAME ) + 1.
  235    L_OK = P_OK+L_OFFSET.
  236  * execute general and TC specific operations                           *
  237    CASE L_OK.
  238      WHEN 'INSR'.                      "insert row
  239        PERFORM FCODE_INSERT_ROW USING    P_TC_NAME
  240                                          P_TABLE_NAME.
  241        CLEAR P_OK.
  242        g_ins_flag = 'X'.
  243
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page      7
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  244      WHEN 'DELE'.                      "delete row
  245
  246        PERFORM FCODE_DELETE_ROW USING    P_TC_NAME
  247                                          P_TABLE_NAME
  248                                          P_MARK_NAME.
  249        CLEAR P_OK.
  250
  251      WHEN 'P--' OR                     "top of list
  252           'P-'  OR                     "previous page
  253           'P+'  OR                     "next page
  254           'P++'.                       "bottom of list
  255        PERFORM COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC USING P_TC_NAME
  256                                              L_OK.
  257        CLEAR P_OK.
  258  *     WHEN 'L--'.                       "total left
  259  *       PERFORM FCODE_TOTAL_LEFT USING P_TC_NAME.
  260  *
  261  *     WHEN 'L-'.                        "column left
  262  *       PERFORM FCODE_COLUMN_LEFT USING P_TC_NAME.
  263  *
  264  *     WHEN 'R+'.                        "column right
  265  *       PERFORM FCODE_COLUMN_RIGHT USING P_TC_NAME.
  266  *
  267  *     WHEN 'R++'.                       "total right
  268  *       PERFORM FCODE_TOTAL_RIGHT USING P_TC_NAME.
  269  *
  270      WHEN 'MARK'.                      "mark all filled lines
  271        PERFORM FCODE_TC_MARK_LINES USING P_TC_NAME
  272                                          P_TABLE_NAME
  273                                          P_MARK_NAME   .
  274        CLEAR P_OK.
  275
  276      WHEN 'DMRK'.                      "demark all filled lines
  277        PERFORM FCODE_TC_DEMARK_LINES USING P_TC_NAME
  278                                            P_TABLE_NAME
  279                                            P_MARK_NAME .
  280        CLEAR P_OK.
  281
  282  *     WHEN 'SASCEND'   OR
  283  *          'SDESCEND'.                  "sort column
  284  *       PERFORM FCODE_SORT_TC USING P_TC_NAME
  285  *                                   l_ok.
  286
  287    ENDCASE.
  288
  289  ENDFORM.                              " USER_OK_TC
  290
  291  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  292  *&      Form  FCODE_INSERT_ROW                                         *
  293  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  294  FORM fcode_insert_row
  295                USING    P_TC_NAME           TYPE DYNFNAM
  296                         P_TABLE_NAME             .
  297
  298  *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
  299    DATA L_LINES_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
  300    DATA L_SELLINE          LIKE SY-STEPL.
  301    DATA L_LASTLINE         TYPE I.
  302    DATA L_LINE             TYPE I.
  303    DATA L_TABLE_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
  304    FIELD-SYMBOLS <TC>                 TYPE CXTAB_CONTROL.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page      8
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  305    FIELD-SYMBOLS <TABLE>              TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  306    FIELD-SYMBOLS <LINES>              TYPE I.
  307  *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
  308
  309    ASSIGN (P_TC_NAME) TO <TC>.
  310
  311  * get the table, which belongs to the tc                               *
  312    CONCATENATE P_TABLE_NAME '[]' INTO L_TABLE_NAME. "table body
  313    ASSIGN (L_TABLE_NAME) TO <TABLE>.                "not headerline
  314
  315  * get looplines of TableControl
  316    CONCATENATE 'G_' P_TC_NAME '_LINES' INTO L_LINES_NAME.
  317    ASSIGN (L_LINES_NAME) TO <LINES>.
  318
  319  * get current line
  320    GET CURSOR LINE L_SELLINE.
  321    if sy-subrc <> 0.                   " append line to table
  322      l_selline = <tc>-lines + 1.
  323  *&SPWIZARD: set top line and new cursor line                           *
  324      if l_selline > <lines>.
  325        <tc>-top_line = l_selline - <lines> + 1 .
  326      else.
  327        <tc>-top_line = 1.
  328      endif.
  329    else.                               " insert line into table
  330      l_selline = <tc>-top_line + l_selline - 1.
  331      l_lastline = <tc>-top_line + <lines> - 1.
  332    endif.
  333  *&SPWIZARD: set new cursor line                                        *
  334    l_line = l_selline - <tc>-top_line + 1.
  335  * insert initial line
  336    INSERT INITIAL LINE INTO <TABLE> INDEX L_SELLINE.
  337    <TC>-LINES = <TC>-LINES + 1.
  338  * set cursor
  339    SET CURSOR LINE L_LINE.
  340
  341    g_i = L_LINE.
  342    g_field = 'ZMM_PREP_ROLEREI-ROLE_NAME'.
  343
  344  ENDFORM.                              " FCODE_INSERT_ROW
  345
  346  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  347  *&      Form  FCODE_DELETE_ROW                                         *
  348  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  349  FORM fcode_delete_row
  350                USING    P_TC_NAME           TYPE DYNFNAM
  351                         P_TABLE_NAME
  352                         P_MARK_NAME   .
  353
  354  *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
  355    DATA L_TABLE_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
  356
  357    FIELD-SYMBOLS <TC>         TYPE cxtab_control.
  358    FIELD-SYMBOLS <TABLE>      TYPE STANDARD TABLE.
  359    FIELD-SYMBOLS <WA>.
  360    FIELD-SYMBOLS <MARK_FIELD>.
  361  *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
  362
  363    ASSIGN (P_TC_NAME) TO <TC>.
  364
  365  * get the table, which belongs to the tc                               *
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page      9
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  366    CONCATENATE P_TABLE_NAME '[]' INTO L_TABLE_NAME. "table body
  367    ASSIGN (L_TABLE_NAME) TO <TABLE>.                "not headerline
  368
  369  * delete marked lines                                                  *
  370    DESCRIBE TABLE <TABLE> LINES <TC>-LINES.
  371
  372    LOOP AT <TABLE> ASSIGNING <WA>.
  373
  374  *   access to the component 'FLAG' of the table header                 *
  375      ASSIGN COMPONENT P_MARK_NAME OF STRUCTURE <WA> TO <MARK_FIELD>.
  376
  377      IF <MARK_FIELD> = 'X' and <WA>+90(1) = ''.
  378        DELETE <TABLE> INDEX SYST-TABIX.
  379        IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
  380          <TC>-LINES = <TC>-LINES - 1.
  381        ENDIF.
  382      ENDIF.
  383    ENDLOOP.
  384
  385  ENDFORM.                              " FCODE_DELETE_ROW
  386
  387  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  388  *&      Form  COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC
  389  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  390  *       text
  391  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  392  *      -->P_TC_NAME  name of tablecontrol
  393  *      -->P_OK       ok code
  394  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  395  FORM COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC USING    P_TC_NAME
  396                                        P_OK.
  397  *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*
  398    DATA L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE     TYPE I.
  399    DATA L_TC_NAME             LIKE FELD-NAME.
  400    DATA L_TC_LINES_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
  401    DATA L_TC_FIELD_NAME       LIKE FELD-NAME.
  402
  403    FIELD-SYMBOLS <TC>         TYPE cxtab_control.
  404    FIELD-SYMBOLS <LINES>      TYPE I.
  405  *-END OF LOCAL DATA----------------------------------------------------*
  406
  407    ASSIGN (P_TC_NAME) TO <TC>.
  408  * get looplines of TableControl
  409    CONCATENATE 'G_' P_TC_NAME '_LINES' INTO L_TC_LINES_NAME.
  410    ASSIGN (L_TC_LINES_NAME) TO <LINES>.
  411  ***********************************************************************
  412    g_tc_lines = <TC>-LINES.
  413  ***********************************************************************
  414
  415  * is no line filled?                                                   *
  416    IF <TC>-LINES = 0.
  417  *   yes, ...                                                           *
  418      L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE = 1.
  419    ELSE.
  420  *   no, ...                                                            *
  421      CALL FUNCTION 'SCROLLING_IN_TABLE'
  422           EXPORTING
  423                ENTRY_ACT             = <TC>-TOP_LINE
  424                ENTRY_FROM            = 1
  425                ENTRY_TO              = <TC>-LINES
  426                LAST_PAGE_FULL        = 'X'
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page     10
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  427                LOOPS                 = <LINES>
  428                OK_CODE               = P_OK
  429                OVERLAPPING           = 'X'
  430           IMPORTING
  431                ENTRY_NEW             = L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE
  432           EXCEPTIONS
  433  *              NO_ENTRY_OR_PAGE_ACT  = 01
  434  *              NO_ENTRY_TO           = 02
  435  *              NO_OK_CODE_OR_PAGE_GO = 03
  436                OTHERS                = 0.
  437    ENDIF.
  438
  439  * get actual tc and column                                             *
  440    GET CURSOR FIELD L_TC_FIELD_NAME
  441               AREA  L_TC_NAME.
  442
  443    IF SYST-SUBRC = 0.
  444      IF L_TC_NAME = P_TC_NAME.
  445  *     set actual column                                                *
  446        SET CURSOR FIELD L_TC_FIELD_NAME LINE 1.
  447      ENDIF.
  448    ENDIF.
  449
  450  * set the new top line                                                 *
  451    <TC>-TOP_LINE = L_TC_NEW_TOP_LINE.
  452
  453
  454  ENDFORM.                              " COMPUTE_SCROLLING_IN_TC
  455

  501  *-BEGIN OF LOCAL DATA--------------------------------------------------*

  514    ASSIGN (L_TABLE_NAME) TO <TABLE>.                "not headerline

  525  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  526  *&      Form  HELP_LIST
  527  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  528  *       text
  529  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  530  *  -->  p1        text
  531  *  <--  p2        text
  532  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  533  FORM HELP_LIST.
  534
  535    if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE is initial.
  536      set cursor field 'ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE'.
  537      message i082(zhelp).
  538      leave to screen 0.
  539    endif.
  540    refresh : it_cond.
  541    concatenate 'FICTR'  'LIKE'  into g_line separated by
  542    space.
  543    concatenate g_line+0(10) '''' ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-CCODE '%' ''''  into
  544                g_line.
  545    append g_line to it_cond.
  546    if help_list_flag <> 'X' .
  547      select * from m_fistb into corresponding fields of table it_m_fistb
  548                    where (it_cond).
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page     12
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  549      SORT IT_M_FISTB BY BEZEICH SPRAS1 BOSSID FIKRS FICTR. help_list_flag
        = 'X'.
  550      refresh it_cond.
  551    endif.
  552    loop at it_m_fistb into wa_m_fistb.
  553  *
  554      if wa_m_fistb-fictr = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-fundc or
  555         wa_m_fistb-fictr = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-fundc2 or
  556         wa_m_fistb-fictr = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-fundc3 or
  557         wa_m_fistb-fictr = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-fundc4.
  558         wa_m_fistb-g_mark = 'X'.
  559      endif.
  560
  561     if old_ok_code = 'DISPLAY' or old_ok_code = 'APPROVE'.
  562        if wa_m_fistb-g_mark = 'X'.
  563          write: / wa_m_fistb-fictr, wa_m_fistb-bezeich.
  564        endif.
  565      else.
  566        write: / wa_m_fistb-g_mark as checkbox, wa_m_fistb-fictr,
  567              wa_m_fistb-bezeich.
  568      endif.
  569
  570      HIDE : wa_m_fistb-g_mark, wa_m_fistb-fictr.
  571  *    CLEAR : wa_m_fistb-g_mark, wa_m_fistb-fictr.
  572    endloop.
  573    lines = sy-linno .
  574
  575  ENDFORM.                    " HELP_LIST

  762  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  763  *&      Form  Save_request
  764  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  765  *       text
  766  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  767  *  -->  p1        text
  768  *  <--  p2        text
  769  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  770  FORM Save_request.
  771
  772    if old_ok_code = 'CREATE' or
  773       old_ok_code = 'CROSSCO' or
  774       old_ok_code = 'CRCROLES'.
  775
  776      perform gen_no.
  777
  778    endif.
  779
  780    if old_ok_code = 'RELEASE' or
  781       old_ok_code = 'APPROVE'.
  782
  783      g_release = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_cr_fl.
  784      g_approve = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app_fl.
  785      g_approve0 = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app0_fl.
  786      g_approve1 = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app1_fl.
  787
  788
  789      select single * from ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ
  790                      where DOCNO = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-docno.
  791
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page     16
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  792      if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_cr_fl is initial.
  793        ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_cr_fl = g_release.
  794      endif.
  795      if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app_fl is initial.
  796        ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app_fl = g_approve.
  797      endif.
  798      if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app1_fl is initial.
  799        ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app1_fl = g_approve1.
  800      endif.
  801
  802      if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app0_fl is initial.
  803        ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app0_fl = g_approve0.
  804      endif.
  805
  806      clear : g_release, g_approve, g_approve0, g_approve1.
  807
  808      if g_release = 'X' and ( g_approve <> 'X' and
  809                               g_approve0 <> 'X' and
  810                               g_approve1 <> 'X' ).
  811
  812        g_app_rel = 'X'.
  813
  814      endif.
  815
  816    endif.
  817
  818  *describe table ist_itemtab lines g_lines_rl.
  819
  820    if old_ok_code = 'RELEASE' and g_lines_rl = 0.
  821      message i089(zhelp).
  822    else.
  823      perform insert_header.
  824    endif.
  825
  826  ENDFORM.                    " Save_request

  844    ENDIF.
  845
  846  ENDFORM.                    " gen_no

 1499  FORM exit_confirm.
 1500
 1501    Data l_choice1.
 1502    clear l_choice1.
 1503
 1504    if old_ok_code = 'CREATE' or
 1505       old_ok_code = 'CROSSCO' or
 1506       old_ok_code = 'CHANGE' or
 1507       old_ok_code = 'DELETE' or
 1508       old_ok_code = 'RELEASE' or
 1509       old_ok_code = 'APPROVE'.
 1510
 1511  " Begin of <RD1K960036>.
 1512  *    CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM_STEP'
 1513  *         EXPORTING
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page     28
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1514  *              TEXTLINE1      = 'Data will be lost, Want to quit? '
 1515  *              TITEL          = 'EXIT'
 1516  *              START_COLUMN   = 25
 1517  *              START_ROW      = 6
 1518  *              CANCEL_DISPLAY = ''
 1519  **                 DEFAULTOPTION = 'N'
 1520  *         IMPORTING
 1521  *              ANSWER         = l_choice1.
 1522
 1523  DATA : l_get2(1) TYPE c.
 1524  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
 1525    EXPORTING
 1526     TITLEBAR                    = 'EXIT '
 1527      TEXT_QUESTION               = 'Data will be lost, Want to quit? '
 1528     DEFAULT_BUTTON              = '2'
 1529     DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON       = ' '
 1530     START_COLUMN                = 25
 1531     START_ROW                   = 6
 1532   IMPORTING
 1533     ANSWER                      = l_get2
 1534   EXCEPTIONS
 1535     TEXT_NOT_FOUND              = 1
 1536     OTHERS                      = 2
 1537            .
 1538  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
 1539         CASE l_get2.
 1540           WHEN '1'.
 1541             MOVE 'J' TO l_choice1.
 1542             WHEN '2'.
 1543               MOVE 'N' TO l_choice1.
 1544               ENDCASE.
 1545               ENDIF.
 1546  " End of <RD1K960036>.
 1547
 1548
 1549      If l_choice1 = 'J'.
 1550        clear l_choice1.
 1551        perform clear.
 1552        perform unlock_record.
 1553        call screen 100.
 1554      else.
 1555      ENDIF.
 1556
 1557    else.
 1558
 1559      perform clear.
 1560      perform unlock_record.
 1561      call screen 100.
 1562
 1563    endif.
 1564
 1565
 1566  ENDFORM.                    " exit_confirm

 1683  *  <--  p2        text
 1684  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1685  FORM text_control_set_text_table1.
 1686
 1687    Refresh: tlinetab1, g_linefrto_itab.
 1688    If old_ok_code <> 'CREATE' or
 1689       old_ok_code = 'CROSSCO' .
 1690      append lines of lines_cors to tlinetab1[].
 1691    Endif.
 1692  *
 1693    loop at tlinetab1[] into g_line132.
 1694      if ( g_line132+0(7) = '* Reply' ) or
 1695         ( g_line132+0(7) = '**Reply' ).
 1696        g_linefrto-line_fr = sy-tabix.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page     31
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1697        g_linefrto-line_to = sy-tabix.
 1698        append g_linefrto to g_linefrto_itab.
 1699        clear: g_linefrto.
 1700      endif.
 1701    endloop.
 1702  *
 1703    call function 'CONVERT_ITF_TO_STREAM_TEXT'
 1704         TABLES
 1705              itf_text    = tlinetab1[]
 1706              text_stream = lt_text_table1.
 1707
 1708    call method gv_text_editor1->set_text_as_stream
 1709         exporting
 1710              text = lt_text_table1
 1711         exceptions
 1712              error_dp        = 1
 1713              error_dp_create = 2
 1714              others          = 3.
 1715  ********************highlight**************************************
 1716    clear g_linefrto.
 1717    loop at g_linefrto_itab into g_linefrto.
 1718      call method gv_text_editor1->HIGHLIGHT_LINES
 1719         exporting
 1720              FROM_LINE = g_linefrto-line_fr
 1721              TO_LINE   = g_linefrto-line_to
 1722              HIGHLIGHT_MODE = 1.
 1723    endloop.
 1724  ********************************************************************
 1725
 1726    if ( old_ok_code = 'CREATE' )
 1727     or ( old_ok_code = 'CROSSCO' )
 1728     or ( old_ok_code = 'CRCROLES' )
 1729     or ( old_ok_code = 'CHANGE' )
 1730     or ( old_ok_code = 'DISPLAY' and zmm_prep_rolereq-comm_fl = 'X' )
 1731   .
 1732      call function 'CONVERT_ITF_TO_STREAM_TEXT'
 1733           TABLES
 1734                itf_text    = tlinetab2
 1735                text_stream = lt_text_table2.
 1736
 1737      call method gv_text_editor2->set_text_as_stream
 1738           exporting
 1739                text = lt_text_table2
 1740           exceptions
 1741                error_dp        = 1
 1742                error_dp_create = 2
 1743                others          = 3.
 1744    endif.

 1746  ENDFORM.                    " text_control_set_text_table1

 1755  FORM save_cors_text.
 1756
 1757    Data: l_theader like thead.

05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1F01                      Page     32
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1758    Data: l_datech(10) type c.

 1761    l_theader-tdobject   = 'ZHELP'.
 1762    l_theader-tdid       = '0001'.
 1763    l_theader-tdspras    =  sy-langu.
 1764    l_theader-tdlinesize =  72.

 1767  *********************************************

 1769      clear g_cores_sender.
 1770      Concatenate sy-datum+6(2) '/'
 1771                  sy-datum+4(2) '/'
 1772                  sy-datum+0(4) into l_datech.
 1773      Concatenate '**Reply' l_datech sy-uname into g_cores_sender
 1774       separated by '          '.
 1775      if NOT TLINETAB2[] IS INITIAL.
 1776        append g_cores_sender to tlinetab1.
 1777      endif.
 1778      clear g_cores_sender.

 1780           EXPORTING
 1781                CLIENT          = SY-MANDT
 1782                HEADER          = l_theader
 1783                SAVEMODE_DIRECT = 'X'
 1784           TABLES
 1785                LINES           = TLINETAB1
 1786           EXCEPTIONS
 1787                ID              = 1
 1788                LANGUAGE        = 2
 1789                NAME            = 3
 1790                OBJECT          = 4
 1791                OTHERS          = 5.
 1792
 1793      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
 1794        MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
 1795                WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
 1796      ENDIF.
 1797    ENDIF.
 1798
 1799  ENDFORM.                    " save_cors_text

    9  MODULE USER_COMMAND_0100 INPUT.
   10
   11  okcode = sy-ucomm.
   12
   13  Case okcode.
   14
   15      When 'BAC' OR 'CAN'.
   16
   17        perform bac_confirm.
   18  *      refresh control 'TABCTRL100' from screen '0100'.
   19        clear okcode.
   20        leave program.
   21
   22      When 'CREATE'.
   23
   24        g_mode = 'CRE'.
   25        clear okcode.
   26
   27      When 'CHANGE'.
   28
   29        g_mode = 'CHA'.
   30        clear okcode.
   31
   32      When 'DISPLAY'.
   33
   34        g_mode = 'DIS'.
   35        clear okcode.
   36
   37      When 'DELETE'.
   38
   39        g_mode = 'DEL'.
   40        clear okcode.
   41
   42      when 'SAVE'.
   43
   44  *        perform check_items.
   45  *        Perform Check_dupl_rec1.
   46          .
   47  *        Perform Save_request.
   48
   49        clear okcode.
   50
   51      when 'RELEASE'.
   52
   53        g_mode = 'REL'.
   54        clear okcode.
   55
   56      when 'APPROVE'.
   57
   58        g_mode = 'APR'.
   59        clear okcode.
   60
   61   ENDCASE.

05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1I01                      Page     67

  451                l_ekgrp+1(1) > 'A'.
  452                  delete it_t024.
  453                endif.

  797  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  798  *&      Module  user_command_100  INPUT
  799  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  800  *       text
  801  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  802  MODULE user_command_100 INPUT.
  803
  804  case okcode_100.
  805
  806      When 'BAC' OR 'CAN'.
  807        perform exit_confirm.
  808      When 'EXT'.
  809        leave program.
  810
  811      When 'CREATE'.
  812
  813        old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  814
  815      When 'CHANGE'.
  816
  817       old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  818
  819      When 'RELEASE'.
  820
  821       old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  822
  823
  824      When 'APPROVE'.
  825
  826       old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  827
  828      When 'COPY'.
  829
  830
  831      When 'DISPLAY'.
  832
  833        old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  834
  835  *    When 'MULTI'.
  836  *
  837  *      call screen 120 STARTING AT 10 5
  838  *                  ENDING   AT 90 15.
  839  *      clear okcode_100.
  840
  841      WHEN 'SAV'.
  842
  843        if old_ok_code = 'DELETE'.
  844
  845            if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-USERIDCR = sy-uname.
  846
  847  *            if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = 'N' or  " 30/05/2006
  848
  849              if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-STATUS = ''.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1I01                      Page     80
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  850                Perform delete_request.
  851              else.
  852                message e138(ZHELP).
  853              endif.
  854            else.
  855              message e056(ZHELP).
  856            endif.
  857        else.
  858          describe table g_TABCTRL100_itab lines g_lines_rl.
  859          if g_lines_rl = 0.
  860             clear okcode_100.
  861             message i140(zhelp).
  862          else.
  863          if old_ok_code = 'RELEASE' and
  864                ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_cr_fl <> 'X'.
  865                message i083(zhelp).
  866
  867          elseif old_ok_code = 'RELEASE' and g_lines_rl = 0.
  868                message i089(zhelp).
  869
  870          elseif old_ok_code = 'APPROVE' and
  871                ( ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app_fl <> 'X' and
  872                ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app0_fl <> 'X' and
  873                ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-req_app1_fl <> 'X' ).
  874                message i087(zhelp).
  875          else.
  876            Perform check_items.
  877  *          Perform check_items_save.
  878            Perform Save_request.
  879          endif.
  880         endif.
  881        endif.
  882
  883      When 'MULTI'.
  884
  885  *      clear help_list_flag.
  886
  887        call screen 120 STARTING AT 10 5
  888                    ENDING   AT 90 15.
  889        clear okcode_100.
  890
  891
  892      WHEN 'DELETE'.
  893
  894         old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  895
  896      WHEN 'ATTACH'.
  897
  898         if old_ok_code = 'CREATE' or
  899            old_ok_code = 'CROSSCO' or
  900            old_ok_code = 'CRCROLES'.
  901            message i137(zhelp).
  902         else.
  903            perform attach_files.
  904            if old_ok_code = 'DISPLAY' and
  905               ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-status = 'IR'.
  906               attach_fl = 'X'.
  907               Perform confirm_more.
  908
  909              If g_choice_more = 'J'.
  910                clear g_choice_more.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1I01                      Page     81
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  911              else.
  912                Perform Save_request.
  913              endif.
  914            endif.
  915         endif.
  916  *       old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  917
  918      WHEN 'LIST'.
  919
  920         perform list_files.
  921
  922  *       old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  923
  924      WHEN 'CORR'.
  925
  926          Call Screen 105 starting at 85 05 ending at 148 24.
  927          clear okcode_100.
  928
  929      WHEN 'CROSSCO'.
  930
  931         old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  932
  933      WHEN 'CRCROLES'.
  934
  935         old_ok_code = okcode_100.
  936
  937      WHEN OTHERS.
  938
  939          clear okcode_100.
  940
  941
  942  endcase.
  943
  944  ENDMODULE.                 " user_command_100  INPUT

  990           IMPORTING

 1650  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1651  *&      Module  validate_lineitem_data  INPUT
 1652  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1653  *       text
 1654  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1655  MODULE validate_lineitem_data INPUT.
 1656
 1657  if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-CROSSCO_FL = 'X' or old_ok_code = 'CROSSCO'.
 1658
 1659  select a~pernr a~begda a~endda a~ename as name a~bukrs  a~werks
 1660               a~persk a~sbmod  c~designo c~r_p_cd c~version
 1661             d~sdesig_text as designation d~adesig_text as adesignation
 1662             d~DISC_CD as DISC_CD
 1663               into corresponding fields of table ist_data
 1664          from ( ( pa0001 as a inner join pa9930 as c

 2316  if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREI-rej_id is initial.

 2325        rej_code = ZMM_PREP_ROLEREI-rej_fl .
 2326      if sy-subrc <> 0.
 2327        g_e_fl = 'X'.
 2328        message e111(zhelp).
 2329      else.
 2330        if g_user = 'L1' and ZMM_PREP_ROLEREI-rej_fl <> 'R'.
 2331          g_e_fl = 'X'.
 2332          message e111(zhelp).
 2333        elseif g_user = 'L3' and ZMM_PREP_ROLEREI-rej_fl <> 'B'.
 2334          g_e_fl = 'X'.
 2335          message e111(zhelp).
 2336        elseif g_user = 'IM' and ZMM_PREP_ROLEREI-rej_fl <> 'I'.
 2337          g_e_fl = 'X'.

 2405        g_srno = g_srno + 1.
 2406        g_TABCTRL100_wa-srno = g_srno.
 2407        modify g_TABCTRL100_itab from g_TABCTRL100_wa.
 2408  endloop.
 2409  describe table g_TABCTRL100_itab  lines g_lines_rl.
 2410  describe table g_TABCTRL100_itab  lines TABCTRL100-lines.
 2411  clear g_srno.
 2412
 2413  ENDMODULE.                 " change_srno  INPUT

 2493               table it_pos where role_type = l_role_type.

 2500   g_field_wa-fieldname = 'CRC_POS'.
 2501   append g_field_wa to g_field_tab.
 2502   g_field_wa-tabname = 'ZMM_PREP_CRCDESG'.
 2503   g_field_wa-fieldname = 'CRC_ORDER_AUTH'.

  168  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  169  MODULE scr100_attr OUTPUT.
  170
  171  CASE old_ok_code.
  172
  173    when ''.
  174
  175       loop at screen.
  176            screen-input = 0.
  177            modify screen.
  178        endloop.
  179
  180    when 'CREATE'.
  181
  182       loop at screen.
  183
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1O01                      Page    112
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  184         if screen-group1 = 'GP1'.
  185             screen-input = 1.
  186             screen-required = 1.
  187             modify screen.
  188         endif.
  189
  190         if screen-group2 = 'GP2'.
  191              screen-required = 0.
  192             modify screen.
  193         endif.
  194
  195         if ( screen-name = 'ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-PERSA' ).
  196             if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-RSN_CODE = '01'.
  197               screen-input = 1.
  198  *             perform pop_up_message.
  199             else.
  200              clear : ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-PERSA, ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-NAME1.
  201              screen-input = 0.
  202             endif.
  203             modify screen.
  204         endif.
  205
  206         if screen-group3 = 'GPC'.
  207             screen-input = 0.
  208             screen-invisible = 1.
  209             modify screen.
  210         endif.
  211
  212         if screen-name = 'ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-REASON1'.
  213           if not ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC is initial.
  214            screen-input = 1.
  215            screen-required = 1.
  216           else.
  217            screen-input = 0.
  218           endif.
  219           modify screen.
  220         endif.
  221
  222      endloop.
  223
  224      when 'CHANGE'.
  225
  226       loop at screen.
  227
  228         if screen-group1 = 'GP1'.

  230             screen-required = 0.
  231             modify screen.
  232         endif.
  233

  235              screen-input = 1.
  236              screen-required = 0.
  237             modify screen.
  238         endif.
  239

  241             if ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-CRC_FL = 'X'.
  242               screen-active = 1.
  243             else.
  244               screen-active = 0.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
05.10.26 OCD      Include MZMMPREPROLE1O01                      Page    113

  245             endif.

  248         endif.
  249

  251              screen-input = 0.
  252              modify screen.
  253         endif.
  254

  256                     g_error_fundc = 'X'.
  257              screen-input = 1.
  258              clear g_error_fundc.
  259              modify screen.
  260         endif.
  261
  262        if screen-name = 'ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-REASON1'.
  263           if not ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC is initial.
  264            screen-input = 1.
  265            screen-required = 1.
  266           else.
  267            screen-input = 0.

  269           modify screen.
  270         endif.

  298         elseif screen-group3 = 'GPC' and ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-CRC_FL <> 'X'.
  299             screen-active = 0.
  300             screen-invisible = 1.
  301             modify screen.
  302         endif.
  303
  304          if screen-name = 'ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-REASON1'.
  305           if not ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-FUNDC is initial.

  306            screen-input = 1.
  307            screen-required = 1.
  308           else.
  309            screen-input = 0.

  311           modify screen.

  314      endloop.

  319

  327              screen-input = 1.
  328              screen-required = 0.
  329             modify screen.
  330         endif.
  331

  333                screen-input = 0.
  334                modify screen.
  335         endif.

  338             screen-name = 'TABCTRL100_INSERT' or

  357                screen-input = 1.

  362             screen-active = 1.

  366             screen-active = 0.

  367             screen-invisible = 1.
  368             modify screen.

  370         endif.
  371

  373            screen-name = 'ZMM_PREP_ROLEREQ-REASON1'.
  374            screen-input = 0.
  375            modify screen.

  393         endif.

  396              screen-required = 0.
  397             modify screen.

 1061        or ( old_ok_code = 'CHANGE' )

 1065    .

 1067      if gv_text_editor2 is initial.
 1068        create object gv_text_editor2
 1069           exporting
 1070                parent = gv_splitter2->bottom_right_container
 1071                wordwrap_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>wordwrap_at_windowborder
 1072                wordwrap_to_linebreak_mode = cl_gui_textedit=>false

 1077                error_dp_create        = 4
 1078                gui_type_not_supported = 5.
 1079          flag2 = 'X'.
 1080      endif.
 1081    endif.

 1083    perform text_control_eingabebereit1.
 1084    perform text_control_set_text_table1.

 1086  ENDMODULE.                 " TEXT_CTRL_VORBEREITEN1  OUTPUT
